##### Attraction/sexuality and childhood romance events
# by Silverswee(e)per

namespace = tianxia

# IDs 2901-3500 reserved

# TIANXIATODO: Pictures, frames
# TIANXIATODO: Trait effects
# TIANXIATODO: AI logic
# TIANXIATODO: Hook into on_actions
# TIANXIATODO: Handle betrothals when setting one up
# TIANXIATODO: Plausibility of pursuing someone, getting jealous, etc.
# TIANXIATODO: Rule out is_unattractive_trigger = yes when it comes to sex_attraction
# TIANXIATODO: Sanity check that follow-ups are sensible if someone has gotten opinion_crush/opinion_childhood_sweetheart or have broken up between events
# TIANXIATODO: Weight multipliers
# TIANXIATODO: Rule out a few more events if you've got a sexuality trait that'd mean you know you are(n't) attracted/interested in someone



##### Events for children

### Bedtime story - 4 to 8 years old

# You were told a story about a boy and a girl that fell in love - you've already realized that's your thing
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2901
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2901
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_told_bedtime_story
		}
		opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2901 # That's always nice!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2907 } # Parent informed
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2934 days = 20 random = 10 } # Follow-up
		}
	}
}

# You were told a story about a boy and a girl that fell in love - you realized that's your thing
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2902
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2902
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_told_bedtime_story
		}
		has_unknown_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2902 # That was nice!
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2907 } # Parent informed
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2934 days = 20 random = 10 } # Follow-up
		}
	}
}

					}
				}

			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2903 # I suppose that was a good story...
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2907 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2903 # Not sure how I felt about that...
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2908 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2903 # Bad story was bad!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2909 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
}

# Parent told child seemed to like the story
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2907
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2907
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			opposite_sex_romance_interest_trait_trigger = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2907 # Good!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			opposite_sex_romance_interest_trait_trigger = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2907 # I guess they like boys/girls...
	}
}

	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
				trait = romance_interest_female
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
				trait = romance_interest_male
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_told_bedtime_story
		}
		has_unknown_same_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
						trait = romance_interest_female
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
						trait = romance_interest_male
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2927 # Yeah, I don't think I like that...
		
		reveal_no_same_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2933 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
}

### Bedtime story follow-up

# Child that liked bedtime story about a boy and a girl falling in love might ask parent that told it if they love the other parent
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2934
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2934
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			at_location = ROOT
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			father_even_if_dead = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			mother_even_if_dead = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			father_even_if_dead = {
				save_event_target_as = other_parent
			}
		}
		else = {
			mother_even_if_dead = {
				save_event_target_as = other_parent
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2934 # I will ask them!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2935 } # Storyteller gets event
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2934 # I don't want to bother them...
		
		prestige = -10
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Conclave"
				}
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						add_trait = timid
					}
					50 = {
						add_trait = brooding
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_trait = shy
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mom/Dad, do you love dad/mom?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2935
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2935
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:other_parent = {
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2935 # Of course I do!
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			fall_deeper_in_love_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2936 } # Child informed of love
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2935 # Come to think of it, I actually do love them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				event_target:other_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
					opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					is_lover = event_target:other_parent
				}
				OR = {
					is_married = event_target:other_parent
					is_consort = event_target:other_parent
				}
			}
			add_lover_and_start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2936 } # Child informed of love
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				root_potentially_interested_in_rivaling_this_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					is_rival = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2935 # I think it's best I pretend to love them...
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue > 5
				}
				mult_modifier=  {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 10
				}
				mult_modifier=  {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 15
				}
				
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = child_believes_you
					FROM = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2940 } # Child falls for lie
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				mult_modifier=  {
					factor = 2
					FROM = {
						intrigue >= 5
					}
				}
				mult_modifier=  {
					factor = 2
					FROM = {
						intrigue >= 10
					}
				}
				
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = child_does_not_believe_you
					FROM = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2943 } # Child falls for lie
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				root_potentially_interested_in_rivaling_this_trigger = no
				OR = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 0
					}
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2935 # We... get along?
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2944 } # Child informed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_2935 # No!
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2945 } # Child informed
		}
	}
}

# Other parent informed by child about love
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2937
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2937
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

		}
	}

					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2937 # And I love them, of course!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = no
				}
			}
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2941 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			fall_deeper_in_love_effect = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {

			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2937 # Actually, I do love them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
					opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					is_lover = event_target:storyteller_parent
				}
				OR = {
					is_married = event_target:other_parent
					is_consort = event_target:other_parent
				}
			}
			add_lover_and_start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2938 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
		else = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2941 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2937 # Well, I don't love them...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2939 } # Inform the storyteller
			}

		else = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2942 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2946 # But I still love them!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {

					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}

		remove_lover_and_end_romance_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {

					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}

### Other child is pretty - opposite sex

# You're kind of finding another child pretty - you already knew you are sexually attracted to the opposite sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2947
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2947
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_pretty_child_event
		}
		opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				age >= 4
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_attractive_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_pretty_child_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
					age >= 4
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 200
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 300
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = attractive_child
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2947 # I'm interested in them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2949 days = 15 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2947 # They're pretty, but I'm not interested in them...
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2949
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2950 } # Known attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2951 } # Discovered attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2952 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2953 } # Known lack of attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2952 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2954 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2957
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2958 } # Known attraction to the same sex
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2959 } # Discovered attraction to the same sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2960 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					no_same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2961 } # Known lack of attraction to the same sex
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2960 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2962 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	max_age = 15

			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no

			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:attractive_child = {
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2964 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}

						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2965 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2966 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2967 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {

					}

						mult_modifier = {

							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2968 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2964 } # Romantic interest and attraction known

					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2966 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2967 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}

# Hidden outcome of being ignored
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2979
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:attractive_child = {
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2980 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_unknown_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
				}
				
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2981 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2983 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2984 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2980 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_unknown_same_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
				}
				
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2981 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2983 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2984 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# The other child seems to be ignoring you - you know you've got romantic feelings for their sex

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2980

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2980 # I will approach them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2985 } # Fire the appropriate event
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2980 # I have a crush, but I don't dare approach them...
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2980 # I don't love them!
	}
}

				is_opposite_sex = event_target:attractive_child
			}

			reveal_same_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Hidden outcome of being avoided
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2995
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:attractive_child = {
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2996 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_unknown_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
				}
				
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2997 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2999 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.3000 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2996 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_unknown_same_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
				}
				
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2997 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2999 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.3000 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2997
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2997 # I have a crush, but they don't seem interested...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_opposite_sex = event_target:attractive_child
			}
			
			reveal_opposite_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		}
		
		else = {
			reveal_same_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2997 # I don't love them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_opposite_sex = event_target:attractive_child
			}
			
			reveal_opposite_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		}
		
		else = {
			reveal_same_sex_romance_interest_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

### Being in the company of another child is nice - opposite sex

# You're kind of finding another child's company nice - you already knew you are romantically attracted to the opposite sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3007
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3007
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_nice_child_event
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				age >= 4
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_attractive_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_nice_child_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
					age >= 4
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 200
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 300
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = attractive_child
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3007 # I'm interested in them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3009 days = 15 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3007 # They're nice, but I'm not interested in them...
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3009
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3010 } # Known attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3011 } # Discovered attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3012 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3013 } # Known lack of attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {

### Being in the company of another child is nice - same sex

# You're kind of finding another child's company nice - you already knew you are romantically attracted to the same sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3015
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3015
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_nice_child_event
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				same_sex = ROOT
				age >= 4
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_attractive_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_nice_child_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					same_sex = ROOT
					age >= 4
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 200
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 300
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = attractive_child
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3015 # I'm interested in them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3017 days = 15 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3015 # They're nice, but I'm not interested in them...
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3017
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3018 } # Known attraction to the same sex
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3019 } # Discovered attraction to the same sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3020 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				no_same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3021 } # Known lack of attraction to the same sex
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3020 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3022 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Hidden event to handle outcome of being approached
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3025
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3026 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3028 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3027 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3026 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					no_same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3028 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3027 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
			}
		}
	}
}

### Fairest in the realm

# Someone of the opposite sex is considered (among) the fairest/most handsome in the realm, and you realize you can see it
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3035
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3035
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 10
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_realm_character = {
			is_adult = yes
			is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
			location = {
				ROOT = {
					location = {
						distance = {
							where = PREVPREV
							value <= 400
						}
					}
				}
			}
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			has_fair_trait_trigger = yes
			is_unattractive_trigger = no # Not maimed/sick/etc.
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_fairest_of_them_all_events
		}
		has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_fairest_of_them_all_events
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				has_fair_trait_trigger = yes
				is_unattractive_trigger = no # Not maimed/sick/etc.
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 20
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 25
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 30
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 35
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 40
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = attractive_character
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3035 # They're attractive, and I'm into them!
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		opinion = {
			name = opinion_infatuated
			who = event_target:attractive_character
		 }
		
		 event_target:attractive_character = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3037 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3035 # They're attractive, but I'm not interested in them...
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
	}
}

# Your body is starting to develop, and those of the opposite sex seem to be getting more interested - you like the attention
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3054
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3054
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 11
	max_age = 14
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		opposite_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		}
		is_unattractive_trigger = no
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			NOR = {
				same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3054 # I like it, and they're pretty!
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3054 # I kind of would like it if I also got attention from the same sex...
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3054 # I don't think they're pretty, however...
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				no_same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_no_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				same_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_interest_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_3054 # I'd like some attention from girls/boys, too, not that either is pretty...
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_same_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = no
				same_sex = ROOT
				age >= 11
				age <= 14
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 100
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 200
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 300
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3059 } # Hidden setup
		}
	}
}

		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		has_unknown_no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_trigger = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		}
		is_unattractive_trigger = no
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3058 # Now, if it was girls/boys paying attention instead...
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			NOR = {
				same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3058 # I'd like attention a bit more if it was from boys/girls, not that they're pretty...
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_same_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				no_same_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_no_same_sex_sex_interest_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3058 # I like it as little as I like attention from the same sex, which is to say not at all
		
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_same_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = no
				same_sex = ROOT
				age >= 11
				age <= 14
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 100
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 200
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 300
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3059 } # Hidden setup
		}
	}
}

# Hidden setup
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3059
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_puberty_events
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3060 } # You already knew you were interested
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_interest_trigger = yes
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3061 } # You figure out you are interested
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3062 } # You already had figured out your lack of interest
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3063 } # You figure out your lack of interest
		}
	}
}

		reveal_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {

			has_unknown_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			OR = {
				no_same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_no_same_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				same_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
				has_unknown_same_sex_sex_interest_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTP_TIANXIA_3061 # I've realized girls and boys are both interesting and not pretty, and I'm not jealous...
		
		reveal_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_no_same_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
		
		reveal_same_sex_sex_interest_effect = yes
	}

### Other child with sweetheart won't shut up about them

# Same sex child won't shut up about their opposite sex sweetheart - you've already figured out you are romantically attracted to the opposite sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3064
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3064
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOT = { # Not if you've currently got a sweetheart
			any_opinion_modifier_target = {
				OR = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
					}
					ROOT_is_former_childhood_sweetheart_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				same_sex = ROOT
				age >= 6
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				any_opinion_modifier_target = {
					is_opposite_sex = PREV
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = PREV
						name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
					}
				}
			}
		}
		opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_got_sweetheart_reaction_event
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = childhood_attraction_romance_sexuality_got_sweetheart_reaction_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					same_sex = ROOT
					age >= 6
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					any_opinion_modifier_target = {
						is_opposite_sex = PREV
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = PREV
							name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = sweetheart_talker
				
				random_opinion_modifier_target = {
					limit = {
						is_opposite_sex = PREV
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = PREV
							name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = sweetheart_talked_about
					save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3064 # I also want a sweetheart!
		
		# TIANXIATODO: Move towards affectionate or something?
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:sweetheart_talked_about = {
				ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3064 # I wish their sweetheart was *my* sweetheart!
		
		# TIANXIATODO: Add crush
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			event_target:sweetheart_talker = {
				ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3064 # I wish they were *my* sweetheart
		
		# TIANXIATODO: Add crush on the correct target
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_friend = event_target:sweetheart_talker
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_3064 # Just shut up already!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:sweetheart_talker
			name = opinion_annoying_child
		}
	}
}

# Proper event setup
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3082
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = no
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3083 } # Known attraction to the opposite sex
			}
			
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3088 } # Bisexual version
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3084 } # Discovered being attracted to the opposite sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3085 } # Not sure
				}
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_unknown_no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
					has_unknown_no_opposite_sex_sex_interest_trigger  =yes
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3087 } # Not interested in the opposite sex
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					same_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
					has_unknown_same_sex_sex_interest_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3089 } # Interested in the same sex
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				no_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
			}
			
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3086 } # Discovered lack of interest in the opposite sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3085 } # Not sure
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3085 } # Not sure
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.3092
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3092
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3092 # Both boys and girls can be very attractive if you ask me!
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			opposite_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3092 # Oh, to wake up next to a pretty boy/girl...
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				same_sex_sex_attraction_known_trigger = yes
				opposite_sex_sex_interest_known_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3092 # They're pretty, yes!
		
		reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction_effect = yes
	}
}

### Childhood marriage promise

# You really love your sweetheart - maybe you should propose?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3100
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3100
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_opinion_modifier_target = {
			age >= 6
			is_adult = no
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
			}
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
			}
			is_married = no
			is_betrothed = no
			no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
			location = {
				ROOT = {
					location = {
						distance = {
							where = PREVPREV
							value <= 400
						}
					}
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_childhood_marriage_promise
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_rejected_childhood_marriage_promise
				}
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_rejected_childhood_marriage_promise
				}
			}
		}
		is_married = no
		is_betrothed = no
		NOT = {
			has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_childhood_marriage_promise
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_opinion_modifier_target = {
			limit = {
				age >= 6
				is_adult = no
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
				}
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
				}
				is_married = no
				is_betrothed = no
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_childhood_marriage_promise
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						name = opinion_rejected_childhood_marriage_promise
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						name = opinion_rejected_childhood_marriage_promise
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = target_sweetheart
		}
		save_event_target_as = proposing_child
		clr_character_flag = patrilineal_marriage
		clr_character_flag = matrilineal_marriage
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			can_marry_patrilineally = yes
			event_target:target_sweetheart = {
				can_marry_patrilineally = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3100 # I will propose a patrilineal marriage!
		
		event_target:target_sweetheart = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3101 } # Sweetheart gets event
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			can_marry_matrilineally = yes
			event_target:target_sweetheart = {
				can_marry_matrilineally = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3100 # I will propose a matrilineal marriage!
		
		event_target:target_sweetheart = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3102 } # Sweetheart gets event
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_opposite_sex = event_target:target_sweetheart
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					can_marry_patrilineally = yes
					event_target:target_sweetheart = {
						can_marry_patrilineally = yes
					}
				}
				AND = {
					can_marry_matrilineally = yes
					event_target:target_sweetheart = {
						can_marry_matrilineally = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3100 # Unfortunately, we wouldn't be able to agree on a marriage type...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			same_sex = event_target:target_sweetheart
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_3100 # Unfortunately, we cannot possibly marry...
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_opposite_sex = event_target:target_sweetheart
			OR = {
				AND = {
					can_marry_patrilineally = yes
					event_target:target_sweetheart = {
						can_marry_patrilineally = yes
					}
				}
				AND = {
					can_marry_matrilineally = yes
					event_target:target_sweetheart = {
						can_marry_matrilineally = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_3100 # Actually, I don't think I want to...
	}
}

# Inform target_sweetheart - positive reply
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3125
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3125
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3125 # Yay!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:proposing_child = {
					is_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:proposing_child = {
						has_character_flag = patrilineal_marriage
					}
				}
				
				tooltip = {
					add_spouse = event_target:proposing_child
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				tooltip = {
					add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:proposing_child
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:proposing_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3126 } # Inform the other child
		}
	}
}

# Inform proposing_child - positive reply
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3126
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3126
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3126 # Until death does us part!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = patrilineal_marriage
			}
			
			add_spouse = FROM
		}
		
		else = {
			add_spouse_matrilineal = FROM
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_ruler = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3126 # Let's hope I also get it approved...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				employer = {
					is_parent_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			employer = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3137 } # Parent informed
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			employer = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3153 } # Non-parent employer informed
			}
		}
	}
}

### Crush not acted on might be acted on

# You have a crush on someone and haven't acted on it (at least not recently) - approach them?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3167
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3167
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_opinion_modifier_target = {
			age >= 6
			is_adult = no
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				name = opinion_crush
			}
			location = {
				ROOT = {
					location = {
						distance = {
							where = PREVPREV
							value <= 400
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_opinion_modifier_target = {
			limit = {
				age >= 6
				is_adult = no
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_crush
				}
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = crush_to_approach
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3167 # I should approach them!
		
		event_target:crush_to_approach = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3168 } # Approached, interest known
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_unknown_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3169 } # Approached, interest discovered
						}
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3170 } # Approached, unsure
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						no_opposite_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3171 } # Approached, no interest known
				}
				else = {
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3172 } # Approached, no interest discovered
						}
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3170 } # Approached, unsure
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3168 } # Approached, interest known
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_unknown_same_sex_romance_attraction_trigger = yes
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3169 } # Approached, interest discovered
						}
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3170 } # Approached, unsure
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						no_same_sex_romance_attraction_known_trigger = yes
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3171 } # Approached, no interest known
				}
				else = {
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3172 } # Approached, no interest discovered
						}
						50 = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.3170 } # Approached, unsure
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3167 # No, I don't want to risk rejection...
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3167 # Actually, I no longer care for them...
		
		# TIANXIATODO: Remove crush
	}
}

### Drifting apart from a sweetheart

# You are not so sure about your feelings for your sweetheart
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3176
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3176
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse?
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_opinion_modifier_target = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
			}
			OR = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					value < 0
				}
				AND = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value < 25
					}
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value > 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_opinion_modifier_target = {
			limit = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					name = opinion_childhood_sweetheart
				}
				OR = {
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value < 0
					}
					AND = {
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < 25
						}
						location = {
							ROOT = {
								location = {
									distance = {
										where = PREVPREV
										value > 400
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3176 # No, I still love them!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				trait = cruel # TIANXIATODO: Better trigger
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3176 # I will break things off gently...
		
		set_character_flag = good_breakup@event_target:changing_relationship_target
		
		tooltip = {
			end_romance_effect = yes # Actually happens later
		}
		
		event_target:changing_relationship_target = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3177 } # Inform sweetheart
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3176 # It's not working out...
		
		set_character_flag = neutral_breakup@event_target:changing_relationship_target
		
		end_romance_effect = yes
		
		event_target:changing_relationship_target = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3181 } # Inform sweetheart
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = cruel # TIANXIATODO: Better trigger
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_3176 # I'm done with them!
		
		set_character_flag = bad_breakup@event_target:changing_relationship_target
		
		end_romance_effect = yes
		
		event_target:changing_relationship_target = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3182 } # Inform sweetheart
		}
	}
}

# Your sweetheart attempted to break up with you kindly
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3177
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3177
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3177 # Are you sure? We're good together...
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3178 } # Reconsider?
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				trait = cruel # TIANXIATODO: Better trigger
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3177 # No hard feelings!
		
		set_character_flag = good_breakup@event_target:changing_relationship_target
		
		end_romance_mutual_effect = yes
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3183 } # Sweetheart took it well
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3177 # I don't like it, but fine!
		
		set_character_flag = neutral_breakup@event_target:changing_relationship_target
		
		end_romance_mutual_effect = yes
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3184 } # Sweetheart took it okay
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = cruel # TIANXIATODO: Better trigger
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_3177 # I hate you!
		
		set_character_flag = bad_breakup@event_target:changing_relationship_target
		
		end_romance_mutual_effect = yes
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3185 } # Sweetheart took it very poorly
		}
	}
}

		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3179 } # It worked
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3178 # No! It's over
		
		clr_character_flag = good_breakup@FROM
		set_character_flag = neutral_breakup@FROM
		
		end_romance_effect = yes
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3180 } # It did not work well
		}
	}
}
